`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Tests/Ui/Components/Recipe/RecipeRowFrameShadowMeshTest.cs`:

```csharp
using Client.Ui.Components.Recipe;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Tests.Ui.Components.Recipe
{
    /// <summary>
    /// レシピ行の光彩と括弧の影のメッシュ（Web の box-shadow 0 0 2px と drop-shadow 0 0 1px）
    /// Meshes for the recipe row glow and bracket shadow (the Web box-shadow 0 0 2px and drop-shadow 0 0 1px)
    /// </summary>
    public class RecipeRowFrameShadowMeshTest
    {
        // Vertex.tint は Color32 なので α を 255 で割って比べ、8 bit の量子化を許す（前例 ProgressArrowGlyphShadowMeshTest）
        // Vertex.tint is Color32, so divide alpha by 255 and allow the 8-bit quantum (precedent ProgressArrowGlyphShadowMeshTest)
        private const float Tolerance = 0.002f + 1f / 255f;

        [Test]
        public void 光彩は線の箱の縁で被覆率半分から外へ2σで消える()
        {
            // 線の箱 (10,10)-(110,30)、σ 1px（ぼかし 2px の半分）、補正済み α 0.0769
            // Border box (10,10)-(110,30), σ 1px (half the 2px blur), corrected alpha 0.0769
            var vertices = new Vertex[RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.GlowVertexCount];
            var indices = new ushort[RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.GlowIndexCount];
            RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.BuildGlow(new Rect(10f, 10f, 100f, 20f), 1f, new Color(0f, 0.804f, 1f, 0.0769f), vertices, indices);
            var outer = RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.RingCount * 4;
            Assert.AreEqual(new Vector3(10f, 10f, Vertex.nearZ), vertices[0].position);
            Assert.AreEqual(new Vector3(8f, 8f, Vertex.nearZ), vertices[outer].position);
            Assert.AreEqual(new Vector3(112f, 32f, Vertex.nearZ), vertices[outer + 2].position);

            // 縁は Web の α 0.25 × 0.5 を明るい色の式で補正した値（0.0769 × 0.5^1.85）、2σ の外端はほぼ 0
            // The edge is Web alpha 0.25 × 0.5 corrected by the light formula (0.0769 × 0.5^1.85); the 2σ rim is nearly 0
            Assert.AreEqual(0.0769f * Mathf.Pow(0.5f, 1.85f), vertices[0].tint.a / 255f, Tolerance);
            Assert.Greater(vertices[0].tint.a, 0, "glow core survives the 8-bit quantum");
            Assert.AreEqual(0, vertices[outer].tint.a);
            AssertIndicesInRange(indices, RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.GlowVertexCount);
        }

        [Test]
        public void 括弧の影は棒の両縁の被覆率の差で塗り外へ2σで消え1pxの棒の内側は裏返らない()
        {
            // 横の棒 9×1、σ 1px、補正済み α 0.1078
            // A 9 by 1 horizontal bar, σ 1px, corrected alpha 0.1078
            var vertices = new Vertex[RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.BarShadowVertexCount];
            var indices = new ushort[RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.BarShadowIndexCount];
            RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.BuildBarShadow(new Rect(0f, 0f, 9f, 1f), 1f, new Color(0.184f, 0.737f, 1f, 0.1078f), vertices, indices);
            var outer = RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.RingCount * 4;

            // 内端は太さの半分の手前（0.49px 内側）で止まり、上下が入れ替わらない
            // The inner end stops 0.49px inside (short of half the thickness), so top and bottom never swap
            Assert.AreEqual(0.49f, vertices[0].position.y, 0.001f);
            Assert.Less(vertices[0].position.y, vertices[3].position.y);
            Assert.AreEqual(new Vector3(-2f, -2f, Vertex.nearZ), vertices[outer].position);

            // 太さ 1px・σ 1px の棒は両縁の差 Φ(-d/σ)−Φ(-(d+1)/σ): 芯（0.49px 内側）は約 0.383、縁は約 0.341、外端はほぼ 0
            // A 1px bar with σ 1px uses the two-edge difference Φ(-d/σ)−Φ(-(d+1)/σ): core (0.49px inside) about 0.383, edge about 0.341, outer rim nearly 0
            Assert.AreEqual(0.1078f * Mathf.Pow(0.38291f, 1.85f), vertices[0].tint.a / 255f, Tolerance);
            Assert.Greater(vertices[0].tint.a, 0, "bracket shadow core survives the 8-bit quantum");
            Assert.AreEqual(0.1078f * Mathf.Pow(0.34134f, 1.85f), vertices[RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.RingCount / 2 * 4].tint.a / 255f, Tolerance);
            Assert.AreEqual(0, vertices[outer].tint.a);
            CollectionAssert.AreEquivalent(new ushort[] { 0, 1, 2, 0, 2, 3 }, new[] { indices[0], indices[1], indices[2], indices[3], indices[4], indices[5] });
            AssertIndicesInRange(indices, RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.BarShadowVertexCount);
        }

        private static void AssertIndicesInRange(ushort[] indices, int vertexCount)
        {
            foreach (var index in indices) Assert.Less(index, vertexCount);
        }
    }
}
```

`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui/Components/Recipe/RecipeRow/RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.cs`:

```csharp
using Client.Ui.Components.Gauges;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Components.Recipe
{
    /// <summary>
    /// レシピ行の光彩（box-shadow 0 0 2px）と括弧の drop-shadow（0 0 1px）を、縁からの距離で刻む矩形の輪の頂点カラーメッシュで組む
    /// Builds the recipe row glow (box-shadow 0 0 2px) and the bracket drop-shadow (0 0 1px) as vertex-colored meshes of rectangle rings stepped by distance from the edge
    /// </summary>
    /// <remarks>
    /// 前例 ProgressArrowGlyphShadowMesh と同じく輪を 8 段で刻み、濃さは Web の α に GaussianEdgeCoverage の被覆率を掛ける（括弧の棒は太さが σ と同じ程度なので両縁の差）。
    /// 2 色とも明るい色（最大成分 0.5 以上）なので α 補正は α^1.85 で、重み w を掛けた補正済みの α は corrected × w^1.85 になる（LinearBlendOpacity は暗い色の式だけを持つ）。
    /// box-shadow のぼかし 2px は σ = 1px（ぼかしの半分）、drop-shadow の 1px は σ そのもの。光彩は線の箱の外だけに出る（外向きの box-shadow は要素の下を塗らない）。
    /// Rings are stepped in eight like the ProgressArrowGlyphShadowMesh precedent, and the alpha is the Web alpha times the GaussianEdgeCoverage coverage (the two-edge difference for bracket bars, whose thickness is about σ).
    /// Both colors are light (largest channel 0.5 or more), so the alpha correction is α^1.85 and a weight w gives corrected × w^1.85 (LinearBlendOpacity only has the dark formula).
    /// A 2px box-shadow blur is σ = 1px (half the blur) and a 1px drop-shadow is σ itself. The glow only shows outside the border box (an outer box-shadow never paints under the element).
    /// </remarks>
    internal static class RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh
    {
        public const int RingCount = 8;
        public const int GlowVertexCount = (RingCount + 1) * 4;
        public const int GlowIndexCount = RingCount * 4 * 6;
        public const int BarShadowVertexCount = (RingCount + 1) * 4;
        public const int BarShadowIndexCount = 6 + RingCount * 4 * 6;

        // 明るい色の α 補正の指数。生成器 translucency.mjs の lightExponent と同じ値にする
        // Exponent of the light-color alpha correction; keep it equal to lightExponent in the generator translucency.mjs
        internal const float LightBlendExponent = 1.85f;

        // 1px の棒の輪を内側へ裏返さない止め（px）
        // Stop that keeps a 1px bar's rings from flipping inward (px)
        private const float BarCenterMargin = 0.01f;

        public static void BuildGlow(Rect borderBox, float sigma, Color glowColor, Vertex[] vertices, ushort[] indices)
        {
            // 線の箱の縁（被覆率 0.5）から外へ 2σ まで刻む
            // Step outward from the border box edge (coverage 0.5) to 2σ
            for (var k = 0; k <= RingCount; k++)
            {
                var distance = 2f * sigma * k / RingCount;
                WriteRing(vertices, k * 4, Grow(borderBox, distance), Tint(glowColor, GaussianEdgeCoverage.At(distance, sigma)));
            }
            var cursor = 0;
            for (var k = 0; k < RingCount; k++) WriteBand(vertices, indices, ref cursor, k * 4);
        }

        public static void BuildBarShadow(Rect bar, float sigma, Color shadowColor, Vertex[] vertices, ushort[] indices)
        {
            // 縁の内外 2σ を刻む。内側は棒の太さの半分の手前で止める（前例の中心線の手前で止める形）
            // Step 2σ inside and outside the edge; inward steps stop short of half the bar thickness (the precedent's stop before the centre line)
            var thickness = Mathf.Min(bar.width, bar.height);
            var innerLimit = BarCenterMargin - thickness * 0.5f;
            for (var k = 0; k <= RingCount; k++)
            {
                var fromEdge = Mathf.Max((2f * k / RingCount - 1f) * 2f * sigma, innerLimit);
                WriteRing(vertices, k * 4, Grow(bar, fromEdge), Tint(shadowColor, BarCoverage(fromEdge, thickness, sigma)));
            }

            // 輪 0 の内側は芯として輪 0 の濃さで塗る
            // The inside of ring 0 fills as the core at ring 0's alpha
            var cursor = 0;
            WriteClockwise(vertices, indices, ref cursor, 0, 1, 2);
            WriteClockwise(vertices, indices, ref cursor, 0, 2, 3);
            for (var k = 0; k < RingCount; k++) WriteBand(vertices, indices, ref cursor, k * 4);
        }

        private static float BarCoverage(float fromEdge, float thickness, float sigma)
        {
            // 細い棒は反対側の縁が σ の内にあるので、半無限の縁の被覆率から反対側の縁の分を引く（1px・σ 1px で芯 0.383）
            // A thin bar's far edge lies within σ, so subtract the far edge from the half-plane coverage (0.383 at the core for 1px and σ 1px)
            return GaussianEdgeCoverage.At(fromEdge, sigma) - GaussianEdgeCoverage.At(fromEdge + thickness, sigma);
        }

        private static Rect Grow(Rect rect, float distance)
        {
            return new Rect(rect.xMin - distance, rect.yMin - distance, rect.width + distance * 2f, rect.height + distance * 2f);
        }

        private static Color Tint(Color correctedColor, float coverage)
        {
            var tint = correctedColor;
            tint.a = correctedColor.a * Mathf.Pow(coverage, LightBlendExponent);
            return tint;
        }

        private static void WriteRing(Vertex[] vertices, int first, Rect rect, Color tint)
        {
            // 左上・右上・右下・左下の順（y 下向き）
            // Top-left, top-right, bottom-right, bottom-left (y down)
            vertices[first] = new Vertex { position = new Vector3(rect.xMin, rect.yMin, Vertex.nearZ), tint = tint };
            vertices[first + 1] = new Vertex { position = new Vector3(rect.xMax, rect.yMin, Vertex.nearZ), tint = tint };
            vertices[first + 2] = new Vertex { position = new Vector3(rect.xMax, rect.yMax, Vertex.nearZ), tint = tint };
            vertices[first + 3] = new Vertex { position = new Vector3(rect.xMin, rect.yMax, Vertex.nearZ), tint = tint };
        }

        private static void WriteBand(Vertex[] vertices, ushort[] indices, ref int cursor, int innerFirst)
        {
            // 隣り合う輪の同じ辺を四角で結ぶ
            // Join the same side of neighbouring rings with a quad
            for (var side = 0; side < 4; side++)
            {
                var innerFrom = (ushort)(innerFirst + side);
                var innerTo = (ushort)(innerFirst + (side + 1) % 4);
                var outerFrom = (ushort)(innerFrom + 4);
                var outerTo = (ushort)(innerTo + 4);
                WriteClockwise(vertices, indices, ref cursor, innerFrom, outerTo, innerTo);
                WriteClockwise(vertices, indices, ref cursor, innerFrom, outerFrom, outerTo);
            }
        }

        private static void WriteClockwise(Vertex[] vertices, ushort[] indices, ref int cursor, ushort a, ushort b, ushort c)
        {
            // 時計回り（y 下向き）の順で書く（前例 ProgressArrowGlyphShadowMesh と同じ判定）
            // Write in clockwise order (y down), with the same test as the ProgressArrowGlyphShadowMesh precedent
            var pa = vertices[a].position;
            var pb = vertices[b].position;
            var pc = vertices[c].position;
            var clockwise = 0f < (pb.x - pa.x) * (pc.y - pa.y) - (pb.y - pa.y) * (pc.x - pa.x);
            indices[cursor++] = a;
            indices[cursor++] = clockwise ? b : c;
            indices[cursor++] = clockwise ? c : b;
        }
    }
}
```

`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui/Components/Recipe/RecipeRow/RecipeRowFrame.cs`:

```csharp
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Theme;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Components.Recipe
{
    /// <summary>
    /// レシピ行の光彩・外周の輪・括弧の影・四隅の括弧を頂点カラーで描く拾わない子（Web の box-shadow 2 層と ::before/::after とその drop-shadow）
    /// Non-picking child drawing the recipe row's glow, outer ring, bracket shadow and corner brackets with vertex colors (the Web two box-shadow layers, ::before/::after and their drop-shadow)
    /// </summary>
    internal sealed class RecipeRowFrame : VisualElement
    {
        public const string UssClassName = "recipe-row__frame";

        private readonly Rect[] _ringRects = new Rect[RecipeRowFrameGeometry.RingRectCount];
        private readonly Rect[] _bracketRects = new Rect[RecipeRowFrameGeometry.BracketRectCount];
        private readonly Vertex[] _glowVertices = new Vertex[RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.GlowVertexCount];
        private readonly ushort[] _glowIndices = new ushort[RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.GlowIndexCount];
        private readonly Vertex[] _barShadowVertices = new Vertex[RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.BarShadowVertexCount];
        private readonly ushort[] _barShadowIndices = new ushort[RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.BarShadowIndexCount];
        private float _outset;
        private float _borderWidth;
        private float _ringWidth;
        private float _bracketLength;
        private float _bracketThickness;
        private Color _ringColor;
        private Color _bracketColor;
        private float _glowBlur;
        private Color _glowColor;
        private float _bracketShadowBlur;
        private Color _bracketShadowColor;
        private bool _hasStyle;

        public RecipeRowFrame()
        {
            AddToClassList(UssClassName);
            pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore;
            generateVisualContent += DrawFrame;
            RegisterCallback<CustomStyleResolvedEvent>(_ => OnCustomStyleResolved());
        }

        private void OnCustomStyleResolved()
        {
            var hasOutset = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadPixels(customStyle, "--recipe-row-frame-outset", out _outset);
            var hasBorder = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadPixels(customStyle, "--recipe-row-frame-border", out _borderWidth);
            var hasRingWidth = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadPixels(customStyle, "--recipe-row-frame-ring-width", out _ringWidth);
            var hasLength = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadPixels(customStyle, "--recipe-row-frame-bracket-length", out _bracketLength);
            var hasThickness = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadPixels(customStyle, "--recipe-row-frame-bracket-thickness", out _bracketThickness);
            var hasRing = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadColor(customStyle, "--recipe-row-frame-ring", out _ringColor);
            var hasBracket = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadColor(customStyle, "--recipe-row-frame-bracket", out _bracketColor);
            var hasGlowBlur = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadPixels(customStyle, "--recipe-row-frame-glow-blur", out _glowBlur);
            var hasGlow = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadColor(customStyle, "--recipe-row-frame-glow", out _glowColor);
            var hasShadowBlur = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadPixels(customStyle, "--recipe-row-frame-bracket-shadow-blur", out _bracketShadowBlur);
            var hasShadow = UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadColor(customStyle, "--recipe-row-frame-bracket-shadow", out _bracketShadowColor);
            _hasStyle = hasOutset && hasBorder && hasRingWidth && hasLength && hasThickness && hasRing && hasBracket && hasGlowBlur && hasGlow && hasShadowBlur && hasShadow;

            // 枠の箱は Web の擬似要素と同じく padding の箱から outset だけ外（USS に負の calc が無いので C# が当てる）
            // The frame box sits outset outside the padding box like the Web pseudo-elements (USS has no negative calc, so C# applies it)
            if (hasOutset) ApplyOutset();
            MarkDirtyRepaint();

            #region Internal

            void ApplyOutset()
            {
                style.left = -_outset;
                style.top = -_outset;
                style.right = -_outset;
                style.bottom = -_outset;
            }

            #endregion
        }

        private void DrawFrame(MeshGenerationContext context)
        {
            // silent-ok: つまみの解決前は描かない / 解決できなければ UssCustomStyleReader が読めなかった名前を LogError で出す
            // silent-ok: draw nothing until the knobs resolve / UssCustomStyleReader logs the unreadable name if they never do
            if (!_hasStyle) return;

            // 重なりは Web と同じ: 光彩の上に輪、括弧の影の上に括弧（Allocate の順が描く順）
            // Same stacking as the Web: the ring over the glow, brackets over their shadow (Allocate order is draw order)
            var size = contentRect.size;
            var borderInset = _outset - _borderWidth;
            RecipeRowFrameGeometry.WriteRingRects(size, borderInset, _ringWidth, _ringRects);
            RecipeRowFrameGeometry.WriteBracketRects(size, _bracketLength, _bracketThickness, _bracketRects);

            // box-shadow のぼかしは σ の 2 倍、drop-shadow のぼかしは σ そのもの
            // A box-shadow blur is twice σ; a drop-shadow blur is σ itself
            var borderBox = new Rect(borderInset, borderInset, size.x - borderInset * 2f, size.y - borderInset * 2f);
            RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.BuildGlow(borderBox, _glowBlur * 0.5f, _glowColor, _glowVertices, _glowIndices);
            WriteMesh(_glowVertices, _glowIndices);
            WriteQuads(_ringRects, _ringColor);
            foreach (var bar in _bracketRects)
            {
                RecipeRowFrameShadowMesh.BuildBarShadow(bar, _bracketShadowBlur, _bracketShadowColor, _barShadowVertices, _barShadowIndices);
                WriteMesh(_barShadowVertices, _barShadowIndices);
            }
            WriteQuads(_bracketRects, _bracketColor);

            #region Internal

            void WriteMesh(Vertex[] vertices, ushort[] indices)
            {
                var mesh = context.Allocate(vertices.Length, indices.Length);
                mesh.SetAllVertices(vertices);
                mesh.SetAllIndices(indices);
            }

            // 画面座標（y 下向き）で時計回りの 2 三角
            // Two clockwise triangles in screen space (y down)
            void WriteQuads(Rect[] rects, Color tint)
            {
                var mesh = context.Allocate(rects.Length * 4, rects.Length * 6);
                var written = 0;
                foreach (var rect in rects)
                {
                    var first = (ushort)written;
                    mesh.SetNextVertex(new Vertex { position = new Vector3(rect.xMin, rect.yMin, Vertex.nearZ), tint = tint });
                    mesh.SetNextVertex(new Vertex { position = new Vector3(rect.xMax, rect.yMin, Vertex.nearZ), tint = tint });
                    mesh.SetNextVertex(new Vertex { position = new Vector3(rect.xMax, rect.yMax, Vertex.nearZ), tint = tint });
                    mesh.SetNextVertex(new Vertex { position = new Vector3(rect.xMin, rect.yMax, Vertex.nearZ), tint = tint });
                    mesh.SetNextIndex(first);
                    mesh.SetNextIndex((ushort)(first + 1));
                    mesh.SetNextIndex((ushort)(first + 2));
                    mesh.SetNextIndex(first);
                    mesh.SetNextIndex((ushort)(first + 2));
                    mesh.SetNextIndex((ushort)(first + 3));
                    written += 4;
                }
            }

            #endregion
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 素材／結果の列を書く**

`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui/Components/Recipe/RecipeRow/RecipeSlotColumn.cs`:

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Components.Recipe
{
    /// <summary>
    /// レシピ行の素材／結果の列。スロットを 2 列で折り返し、列に収まらなければ開発者へ知らせる
    /// Material/result column of a recipe row; wraps slots in two columns and tells developers when they do not fit
    /// </summary>
    /// <remarks>
    /// Web の recipeSlotLayout は列数を 1〜2 にし、スロットの寸法を列の幅から縮める（cqw）。UITK はカスタムプロパティを C# から書けないので寸法は USS の上限のまま置き、はみ出しを LogError で知らせる。
    /// The Web recipeSlotLayout uses one or two columns and shrinks slots to the column width (cqw). UITK cannot set custom properties from C#, so slots keep the USS maximum and an overflow is reported through LogError.
    /// </remarks>
    internal sealed class RecipeSlotColumn : VisualElement
    {
        // Web の MAX_SLOT_COLUMNS（recipeSlotLayout.ts）
        // Web MAX_SLOT_COLUMNS (recipeSlotLayout.ts)
        private const int MaxSlotColumns = 2;

        // レイアウトの小数の丸めをはみ出しと数えない幅
        // Width that layout rounding may exceed without counting as overflow
        private const float OverflowTolerancePixels = 0.5f;

        private readonly string _rowTestid;
        private readonly VisualElement _grid;
        private bool _overflowReported;

        public RecipeSlotColumn(string rowTestid, string sideUssClassName)
        {
            _rowTestid = rowTestid;
            AddToClassList("recipe-row__slots");
            AddToClassList(sideUssClassName);
            pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore;
            _grid = NewBox("recipe-row__slot-grid");
            Add(_grid);

            // 列と格子のどちらの寸法が変わっても収まりを見直す
            // Re-check the fit whenever either the column or the grid changes size
            RegisterCallback<GeometryChangedEvent>(_ => ReportOverflow());
            _grid.RegisterCallback<GeometryChangedEvent>(_ => ReportOverflow());
        }

        public void SetSlots(IReadOnlyList<VisualElement> slots)
        {
            // 列数は 1〜2。3 点以上は列を増やさず行を増やす（裁定 2026-08-20）
            // One or two columns; from three slots on, add rows rather than columns (ruling 2026-08-20)
            _grid.Clear();
            var columns = Math.Max(1, Math.Min(slots.Count, MaxSlotColumns));
            for (var start = 0; start < slots.Count; start += columns)
            {
                var line = NewBox("recipe-row__slot-line");
                line.EnableInClassList("recipe-row__slot-line--after", start > 0);
                _grid.Add(line);
                for (var i = start; i < Math.Min(start + columns, slots.Count); i++) line.Add(NewCell(slots[i], i > start));
            }

            #region Internal

            // 間隔は 2 点目以降の枠の左の margin で付ける（受けたスロットの要素には触らない）
            // Gaps come from the left margin of the cells after the first (the received slot elements stay untouched)
            VisualElement NewCell(VisualElement slot, bool afterFirst)
            {
                var cell = NewBox("recipe-row__slot-cell");
                cell.EnableInClassList("recipe-row__slot-cell--after", afterFirst);
                cell.Add(slot);
                return cell;
            }

            #endregion
        }

        private void ReportOverflow()
        {
            // 格子が列より広いと、Web なら縮むところがはみ出す。はみ出しに入った時だけ 1 回知らせる
            // A grid wider than the column overflows where the Web would shrink; report once when it starts overflowing
            var overflows = _grid.layout.width > contentRect.width + OverflowTolerancePixels;
            if (overflows && !_overflowReported) Debug.LogError($"[RecipeRow] {_rowTestid}: slots overflow the column ({_grid.layout.width:0.#}px > {contentRect.width:0.#}px); the Web shrinks them with container queries, UITK keeps --recipe-slot-size-max (look-exception: LX-91)");
            _overflowReported = overflows;
        }

        private static VisualElement NewBox(string ussClassName)
        {
            var box = new VisualElement { pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore };
            box.AddToClassList(ussClassName);
            return box;
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 行の本体を書く**

`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui/Components/Recipe/RecipeRow/RecipeRow.cs`:

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Client.Ui.Components.Gauges;
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Text;
using Client.Ui.State.Text;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Components.Recipe
{
    /// <summary>
    /// レシピ行。素材・中央列（秒数→矢印→操作）・結果の 3 列と、外周の輪と四隅の括弧
    /// Recipe row: materials, the center column (duration, arrow, action) and results, with the outer ring and corner brackets
    /// </summary>
    /// <remarks>
    /// スロットは画面が作った要素を受けて並べるだけで、操作は画面がスロットを直接購読する（Web は ReactNode[] を受ける）。
    /// It only lays out slot elements the screen built; the screen subscribes to the slots directly (the Web takes ReactNode[]).
    /// </remarks>
    public sealed class RecipeRow : VisualElement
    {
        public const string UssClassName = "recipe-row";
        private const string ActionShownUssClassName = "recipe-row__action--shown";

        private readonly RecipeSlotColumn _materials;
        private readonly RecipeSlotColumn _results;
        private readonly LocalizedLabel _duration;
        private readonly ProgressArrowGlyph _arrow;
        private readonly VisualElement _action;

        public RecipeRow(string testid, string arrowTestid)
        {
            name = testid;
            AddToClassList(UssClassName);

            // 枠の飾りは最初の子にして中身より下に描く
            // The frame decoration is the first child so it draws below the contents
            Add(new RecipeRowFrame());

            // 素材・中央列・結果の 3 列（Web の grid の 1fr・ボタン幅・1fr）
            // Three columns: materials, center and results (the Web grid's 1fr, button width, 1fr)
            _materials = new RecipeSlotColumn(testid, "recipe-row__materials");
            Add(_materials);
            var center = new VisualElement();
            center.AddToClassList("recipe-row__center");
            Add(center);
            _results = new RecipeSlotColumn(testid, "recipe-row__results");
            Add(_results);

            // 中央列は秒数→矢印→操作の縦積み（裁定 2026-08-20）。操作が無ければ操作段を描かない（裁定 2026-09-05）
            // The center stacks duration, arrow and action (ruling 2026-08-20); no action means no action slot (ruling 2026-09-05)
            _duration = new LocalizedLabel { name = $"{testid}-duration", pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore };
            _duration.AddToClassList("recipe-row__duration");
            center.Add(_duration);
            _arrow = new ProgressArrowGlyph(arrowTestid);
            _arrow.AddToClassList("recipe-row__arrow");
            center.Add(_arrow);
            _action = new VisualElement();
            _action.AddToClassList("recipe-row__action");
            center.Add(_action);
        }

        public void SetSlots(IReadOnlyList<VisualElement> materials, IReadOnlyList<VisualElement> results)
        {
            _materials.SetSlots(materials);
            _results.SetSlots(results);
        }

        public void SetDuration(UiText duration)
        {
            _duration.SetText(duration);
        }

        public void SetProgress(float ratio01)
        {
            _arrow.SetProgress(ratio01);
        }

        public void SetIdle()
        {
            _arrow.SetIdle();
        }

        public void SetAction(VisualElement action)
        {
            _action.Clear();
            _action.Add(action);
            _action.AddToClassList(ActionShownUssClassName);
        }

        public void ClearAction()
        {
            _action.Clear();
            _action.RemoveFromClassList(ActionShownUssClassName);
        }
    }
}
```

`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui/Components/Recipe/RecipeRow/RecipeRow.uss`:

```css
/* web: moorestech_web/webui/src/features/recipe/views/RecipeBox.module.css の .recipeBox・.recipeMaterials・.recipeResult・.recipeArrowCol・.recipeDuration・.recipeActionSlot と ::before/::after（components.md §8.17） */
/* web: .recipeBox, .recipeMaterials, .recipeResult, .recipeArrowCol, .recipeDuration, .recipeActionSlot and ::before/::after in moorestech_web/webui/src/features/recipe/views/RecipeBox.module.css (components.md §8.17) */
.recipe-row {
    --recipe-row-border-width: 1px;
    flex-direction: row;
    align-items: center;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    padding: var(--recipe-row-padding-block) var(--recipe-row-padding-inline);
    border-width: var(--recipe-row-border-width);
    border-color: var(--recipe-row-border);
    border-radius: 0;
    background-color: var(--recipe-row-face);
}

/* 光彩・外周の輪・四隅の括弧とその影（Web の box-shadow 0 0 0 1.2px と 0 0 2px、::before/::after と drop-shadow 0 0 1px）。位置（padding の箱から外へ outset）は C# が当てる */
/* Glow, outer ring, corner brackets and their shadow (Web box-shadow 0 0 0 1.2px and 0 0 2px, ::before/::after and drop-shadow 0 0 1px); C# places the box outset outside the padding box */
.recipe-row__frame {
    position: absolute;
    --recipe-row-frame-outset: 3px;
    --recipe-row-frame-border: var(--recipe-row-border-width);
    --recipe-row-frame-ring-width: 1.2px;
    --recipe-row-frame-ring: var(--recipe-row-ring);
    --recipe-row-frame-bracket-length: 9px;
    --recipe-row-frame-bracket-thickness: 1px;
    --recipe-row-frame-bracket: var(--recipe-row-bracket);
    --recipe-row-frame-glow-blur: 2px;
    --recipe-row-frame-glow: var(--recipe-row-glow);
    --recipe-row-frame-bracket-shadow-blur: 1px;
    --recipe-row-frame-bracket-shadow: var(--recipe-row-bracket-shadow);
}

/* 素材・結果の列は等幅（Web の minmax(0, 1fr)）。スロットの寸法は上限のまま（Web の cqw の縮みは写さない） */
/* Material and result columns share the width (Web minmax(0, 1fr)); slots keep the maximum size (the Web cqw shrink is not copied) */
.recipe-row__slots {
    flex-grow: 1;
    flex-shrink: 1;
    flex-basis: 0;
    min-width: 0;
    flex-direction: row;
    align-items: center;
    --slot-size: var(--recipe-slot-size-max);
    --icon-pad: 1px;
    --count-bottom: -1px;
    --count-font-size: 16px;
    --count-letter-spacing: 12px;
}

/* 素材は左寄せで、所持/必要の文字はスロットの外へ出さない（Web の .materialSlot） */
/* Materials align left and keep owned/required text inside the slot (Web .materialSlot) */
.recipe-row__materials {
    justify-content: flex-start;
    --shortage-count-right: 0px;
    --shortage-count-bottom: 0px;
}

.recipe-row__results {
    justify-content: flex-end;
}

/* 格子は中身の幅のまま縮めない（はみ出しを C# が測る） */
/* The grid keeps its content width without shrinking (C# measures the overflow) */
.recipe-row__slot-grid,
.recipe-row__slot-line,
.recipe-row__slot-cell {
    flex-shrink: 0;
}

.recipe-row__slot-line {
    flex-direction: row;
}

.recipe-row__slot-line--after {
    margin-top: var(--recipe-slot-gap);
}

.recipe-row__slot-cell--after {
    margin-left: var(--recipe-slot-gap);
}

/* 中央列はクラフトボタンの幅で固定し、左右に列の間隔（裁定 2026-08-20） */
/* The center column is fixed to the craft button width with the column gap on both sides (ruling 2026-08-20) */
.recipe-row__center {
    width: var(--recipe-craft-button-width);
    flex-shrink: 0;
    margin-top: 0;
    margin-bottom: 0;
    margin-left: var(--recipe-column-gap);
    margin-right: var(--recipe-column-gap);
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
}

/* 秒数。Web の line-height: 1 は OutlinedCountLabel と同じく高さを字の大きさにして基線を 18.1% 下げる */
/* Duration; the Web line-height: 1 sets the height to the font size and lowers the baseline by 18.1%, as OutlinedCountLabel does */
.recipe-row__duration {
    max-width: 100%;
    height: var(--recipe-duration-text-size);
    margin: 0;
    padding: 0;
    font-size: var(--recipe-duration-text-size);
    color: var(--text-high-contrast);
    -unity-text-align: lower-center;
    translate: 0 18.1%;
    white-space: nowrap;
    overflow: hidden;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
}

.recipe-row__arrow {
    flex-shrink: 0;
    margin-top: 2px;
}

/* 操作段は操作があるときだけ描き、最小の高さはボタンの高さ */
/* The action slot is drawn only with an action; its minimum height is the button height */
.recipe-row__action {
    display: none;
    margin-top: 2px;
    min-height: var(--recipe-action-button-height);
    flex-direction: row;
    align-items: flex-start;
    justify-content: center;
}

.recipe-row__action--shown {
    display: flex;
}
```


`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Tests/Ui/Components/Recipe/RecipeRowLayoutTest.cs`:

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using Client.Localization;
using Client.Tests.Ui.TestHost;
using Client.Ui.Components.Gauges;
using Client.Ui.Components.Recipe;
using Client.Ui.Components.Slots;
using Client.Ui.State.Text;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;
using static Client.Tests.Ui.Components.ThemeTokenFile;

namespace Client.Tests.Ui.Components.Recipe
{
    /// <summary>
    /// RecipeRow の名前・折り返し・列の幅・寸法の上書き・操作段・はみ出しの報告（L1）
    /// RecipeRow names, wrapping, column widths, size overrides, action slot and overflow report (L1)
    /// </summary>
    public class RecipeRowLayoutTest : UiTestHost
    {
        private const string ProbePath = "Assets/Scripts/Client.Tests/Ui/Components/Recipe/RecipeKnobOverrides.uss";
        private const string TestId = "recipe-row-test";
        private const string ArrowTestId = "recipe-row-test-arrow";
        private const string IdleClassName = "progress-arrow-glyph--idle";

        [SetUp]
        public void LoadDictionary()
        {
            Localize.Initialize();
        }

        [Test]
        public void 名前と秒数と矢印はWebのtestidで矢印は静止から始まり進捗で切り替わる()
        {
            var row = Mount(rootVisualElement, 1, 1, 600f);
            Assert.AreEqual(TestId, row.name);
            Assert.AreEqual("5秒", row.Q<Label>($"{TestId}-duration").text);
            var arrow = row.Q<ProgressArrowGlyph>(ArrowTestId);
            Assert.IsTrue(arrow.ClassListContains(IdleClassName));
            row.SetProgress(0.5f);
            Assert.IsFalse(arrow.ClassListContains(IdleClassName));
            row.SetIdle();
            Assert.IsTrue(arrow.ClassListContains(IdleClassName));
        }

        [Test]
        public void 素材と結果は2列で折り返し素材は左へ結果は右へ寄る()
        {
            var row = Mount(rootVisualElement, 3, 3, 600f);
            var pitch = ResolvePixels("--recipe-slot-size-max") + ResolvePixels("--recipe-slot-gap");

            // 素材: 1 行目に 2 点、2 行目の 1 点は左の列。列の左端から並ぶ
            // Materials: two on the first line, the third in the left column below, starting at the column's left edge
            Assert.AreEqual(row.Q(className: "recipe-row__materials").worldBound.xMin, Bound(row, "m-0").xMin, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(Bound(row, "m-0").xMin + pitch, Bound(row, "m-1").xMin, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(Bound(row, "m-0").yMin, Bound(row, "m-1").yMin, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(Bound(row, "m-0").yMin + pitch, Bound(row, "m-2").yMin, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(Bound(row, "m-0").xMin, Bound(row, "m-2").xMin, 0.5f);

            // 結果: 格子ごと右へ寄り、2 行目の 1 点は格子の左の列（Web の justify-content: end の grid）
            // Results: the whole grid aligns right and the third sits in the grid's left column (the Web justify-content: end grid)
            Assert.AreEqual(row.Q(className: "recipe-row__results").worldBound.xMax, Bound(row, "r-1").xMax, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(Bound(row, "r-0").xMin, Bound(row, "r-2").xMin, 0.5f);
        }

        [Test]
        public void 中央列はクラフトボタンの幅で左右に列の間隔をとり矢印は素材の点数によらず行の中央()
        {
            var row = Mount(rootVisualElement, 1, 2, 600f);
            var center = row.Q(className: "recipe-row__center");
            Assert.AreEqual(ResolvePixels("--recipe-craft-button-width"), center.layout.width, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(ResolvePixels("--recipe-column-gap"), center.resolvedStyle.marginLeft, 0.01f);
            Assert.AreEqual(ResolvePixels("--recipe-column-gap"), center.resolvedStyle.marginRight, 0.01f);
            Assert.AreEqual(row.Q(className: "recipe-row__materials").layout.width, row.Q(className: "recipe-row__results").layout.width, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(row.worldBound.center.x, row.Q(ArrowTestId).worldBound.center.x, 0.5f);
        }

        [Test]
        public void スロットは上限の寸法で機械のレシピ選択の上書きでは32pxと狭いpaddingになる()
        {
            var plain = Mount(rootVisualElement, 1, 1, 600f);
            Assert.AreEqual(ResolvePixels("--recipe-slot-size-max"), Bound(plain, "m-0").width, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(ResolvePixels("--recipe-row-padding-block"), plain.resolvedStyle.paddingTop, 0.01f);
            plain.RemoveFromHierarchy();

            var probe = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<StyleSheet>(ProbePath);
            Assert.IsNotNull(probe, ProbePath);
            var list = new VisualElement();
            list.styleSheets.Add(probe);
            list.AddToClassList("recipe-machine-selection-probe");
            rootVisualElement.Add(list);
            var row = Mount(list, 1, 1, 600f);
            Assert.AreEqual(32f, Bound(row, "m-0").width, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(5.6f, row.resolvedStyle.paddingTop, 0.01f);
        }

        [Test]
        public void 操作が無ければ操作段を描かずSetActionで矢印の下2pxに置く()
        {
            var row = Mount(rootVisualElement, 1, 1, 600f);
            var action = row.Q(className: "recipe-row__action");
            Assert.AreEqual(DisplayStyle.None, action.resolvedStyle.display);

            // 操作の高さ 10px でも段はボタンの高さを最小に持つ
            // Even a 10px action keeps the slot at the button height minimum
            var probe = new VisualElement { name = "action-probe" };
            probe.style.width = 40f;
            probe.style.height = 10f;
            row.SetAction(probe);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.AreEqual(DisplayStyle.Flex, action.resolvedStyle.display);
            Assert.AreEqual(ResolvePixels("--recipe-action-button-height"), action.layout.height, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(row.Q(ArrowTestId).worldBound.yMax + 2f, action.worldBound.yMin, 0.5f);

            row.ClearAction();
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.AreEqual(DisplayStyle.None, action.resolvedStyle.display);
            Assert.IsNull(row.Q("action-probe"));
        }

        [Test]
        public void 列に収まらないスロットは縮めず開発者へエラーで知らせる()
        {
            // 幅 200 では列が約 38.8px。素材 1 点は収まり、結果 2 点（83.6px）がはみ出す
            // At width 200 each column is about 38.8px; one material fits and two results (83.6px) overflow
            LogAssert.Expect(LogType.Error, new Regex(@"\[RecipeRow\] recipe-row-test: slots overflow"));
            var row = Mount(rootVisualElement, 1, 2, 200f);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.AreEqual(ResolvePixels("--recipe-slot-size-max"), Bound(row, "r-1").width, 0.5f);
        }

        private RecipeRow Mount(VisualElement parent, int materialCount, int resultCount, float width)
        {
            // 秒数は載せる前に渡す（文の無い LocalizedLabel は 1 フレーム後に LogError を出す）
            // Pass the duration before mounting (a LocalizedLabel without text logs an error one frame later)
            var row = new RecipeRow(TestId, ArrowTestId);
            row.style.width = width;
            row.SetDuration(UiText.FromVerbatim("5秒"));
            row.SetSlots(Slots("m", materialCount), Slots("r", resultCount));
            parent.Add(row);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            return row;
        }

        private static IReadOnlyList<VisualElement> Slots(string prefix, int count)
        {
            var slots = new List<VisualElement>();
            // 空のスロットでも枠の寸法は --slot-size で決まる
            // An empty slot still takes its frame size from --slot-size
            for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) slots.Add(new ItemSlot($"{prefix}-{i}"));
            return slots;
        }

        private static Rect Bound(VisualElement row, string slotTestid)
        {
            return row.Q(slotTestid).worldBound;
        }
    }
}
```

`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Tests/Ui/Components/Recipe/RecipeRowContentTest.cs`:

```csharp
using Client.Localization;
using Client.Tests.Ui.TestHost;
using Client.Ui.Components.Recipe;
using Client.Ui.Components.Slots;
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Icons;
using Client.Ui.State.Text;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;
using static Client.Tests.Ui.Components.ThemeTokenFile;

namespace Client.Tests.Ui.Components.Recipe
{
    /// <summary>
    /// RecipeRow に載る液体（Web の機械のレシピ選択行）・線と面と枠の色と位置・描画の健全性（L1）
    /// Fluids in a RecipeRow (the Web machine recipe selection row), border/face/frame colors and placement, and drawing sanity (L1)
    /// </summary>
    public class RecipeRowContentTest : UiTestHost
    {
        private const string RecipeGuid = "84000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";

        [SetUp]
        public void LoadDictionary()
        {
            Localize.Initialize();
        }

        [Test]
        public void 入出力の液体は素材と結果の列にFluidAmountSlotの枠として量付きで並ぶ()
        {
            // 機械のレシピ選択の行と同じ組み方: 入力の液体 10・出力の液体 1000 を Web と同じ testid の枠で渡す
            // Built like the machine recipe selection row: input fluid 10 and output fluid 1000 in frames with the Web testids
            var inputId = $"machine-recipe-{RecipeGuid}-input-fluid-0";
            var outputId = $"machine-recipe-{RecipeGuid}-output-fluid-0";
            var input = new FluidAmountSlot(inputId);
            input.SetFluid(Icon("87000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001"), SlotAmountBadge.Show(10));
            var output = new FluidAmountSlot(outputId);
            output.SetFluid(Icon("87000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002"), SlotAmountBadge.Show(1000));
            var row = new RecipeRow($"machine-recipe-{RecipeGuid}-row", $"machine-recipe-{RecipeGuid}-arrow");
            row.style.width = 600f;
            row.SetDuration(UiText.FromVerbatim("7秒"));
            row.SetSlots(new VisualElement[] { input }, new VisualElement[] { output });
            rootVisualElement.Add(row);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();

            var materials = row.Q(className: "recipe-row__materials");
            var results = row.Q(className: "recipe-row__results");
            Assert.IsNotNull(materials.Q(inputId), inputId);
            Assert.IsNotNull(results.Q(outputId), outputId);
            Assert.AreEqual(SlotAmountFormat.Format(10), AmountText(materials, inputId));
            Assert.AreEqual(SlotAmountFormat.Format(1000), AmountText(results, outputId));
        }

        [Test]
        public void 線と面はα補正したトークンで枠は線の箱から2px外へ広がり入力を拾わない()
        {
            var row = MountPlain();
            Assert.AreEqual(1f, row.resolvedStyle.borderTopWidth, 0.01f);
            AssertSameColor(ResolveColor("--recipe-row-border"), row.resolvedStyle.borderTopColor, "border");
            AssertSameColor(ResolveColor("--recipe-row-face"), row.resolvedStyle.backgroundColor, "face");

            // 枠の箱は padding の箱から 3px 外＝線の箱から 2px 外（Web の ::before/::after）
            // The frame box is 3px outside the padding box, i.e. 2px outside the border box (Web ::before/::after)
            var frame = row.Q(className: "recipe-row__frame");
            Assert.AreEqual(PickingMode.Ignore, frame.pickingMode);
            Assert.AreEqual(row.worldBound.xMin - 2f, frame.worldBound.xMin, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(row.worldBound.yMin - 2f, frame.worldBound.yMin, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(row.worldBound.xMax + 2f, frame.worldBound.xMax, 0.5f);
            Assert.AreEqual(row.worldBound.yMax + 2f, frame.worldBound.yMax, 0.5f);
            AssertSameColor(ResolveColor("--recipe-row-ring"), FrameColor(frame, "--recipe-row-frame-ring"), "ring");
            AssertSameColor(ResolveColor("--recipe-row-bracket"), FrameColor(frame, "--recipe-row-frame-bracket"), "bracket");
            AssertSameColor(ResolveColor("--recipe-row-glow"), FrameColor(frame, "--recipe-row-frame-glow"), "glow");
            AssertSameColor(ResolveColor("--recipe-row-bracket-shadow"), FrameColor(frame, "--recipe-row-frame-bracket-shadow"), "bracket shadow");
        }

        [Test]
        public void 描画でエラーを出さない()
        {
            var row = MountPlain();
            row.SetProgress(0.42f);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            LogAssert.NoUnexpectedReceived();
        }

        private RecipeRow MountPlain()
        {
            var row = new RecipeRow("recipe-row-content-test", "recipe-row-content-test-arrow");
            row.style.width = 600f;
            row.SetDuration(UiText.FromVerbatim("5秒"));
            row.SetSlots(new VisualElement[] { new ItemSlot("m-0") }, new VisualElement[] { new ItemSlot("r-0") });
            rootVisualElement.Add(row);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            return row;
        }

        private static UiIcon Icon(string idText)
        {
            return new UiIcon(Texture2D.whiteTexture, idText, UiText.FromVerbatim(idText));
        }

        private static string AmountText(VisualElement column, string frameTestid)
        {
            // 量の帯は C4-a の FluidAmountSlot が name = 枠の testid + "-amount" で置く（マージ後に実物と照合: 文字の層の取り方は C4-a の FluidAmountSlotTest に合わせる）
            // C4-a's FluidAmountSlot names the amount band frame testid + "-amount" (check against the merged code: read the glyph layer as C4-a's FluidAmountSlotTest does)
            var amount = column.Q($"{frameTestid}-amount");
            Assert.IsNotNull(amount, $"{frameTestid}-amount");
            return amount.Q<Label>().text;
        }

        private static Color FrameColor(VisualElement frame, string property)
        {
            Assert.IsTrue(frame.customStyle.TryGetValue(new CustomStyleProperty<Color>(property), out var color), property);
            return color;
        }
    }
}
```

（`MountPlain` は枠の位置を `CustomStyleResolvedEvent` で当てた後のレイアウトを待つため 2 フレーム進める）

```csharp
using Client.Localization;
using Client.Tests.Ui.TestHost;
using Client.Ui.Components.Recipe;
using Client.Ui.State.Recipe.Data;
using Client.Ui.State.Recipe.Logic;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;
using static Client.Tests.Ui.Components.ThemeTokenFile;
using static Client.Tests.Ui.State.Recipe.RecipeFixtures;

namespace Client.Tests.Ui.Components.Recipe
{
    /// <summary>
    /// レベル継承のバッジの表示と隠しと見た目（部品の L1。Web の 4・5 件目の結線は S4-machine が写す）
    /// Level inheritance badge show/hide and look (component L1; the Web tests 4 and 5 wiring is copied by S4-machine)
    /// </summary>
    public class LevelInheritanceBadgeTest : UiTestHost
    {
        private const string TestId = "level-inheritance-badge-test";

        [SetUp]
        public void LoadDictionary()
        {
            Localize.Initialize();
        }

        [Test]
        public void 継承の文を渡すとバッジの文を出す()
        {
            // Web と同じ継承（入力 0 → 出力 0・解放済みの上限 2）から文を作って載せる
            // Build the text from the same inheritance as the Web test (input 0 to output 0, unlocked cap 2) and show it
            var inheritance = RecipeLevelInheritance.InheritUpToUnlocked(0, Ids(11, 12, 13), 0, Ids(21, 22, 23), 2);
            Assert.IsTrue(LevelInheritanceBadgeText.TryBuild(inheritance, out var text));
            var badge = new LevelInheritanceBadge(TestId);
            badge.Show(text);
            rootVisualElement.Add(badge);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();

            Assert.AreEqual(DisplayStyle.Flex, badge.resolvedStyle.display);
            Assert.AreEqual(text.Resolve(), rootVisualElement.Q<Label>(TestId).text);
        }

        [Test]
        public void 隠すとtestidの要素が木から無くなる()
        {
            // Web は継承しないとき null を返して testid の要素ごと描かない。出していたバッジを隠しても同じになる
            // The Web returns null and renders no testid element without inheritance; hiding a shown badge matches that
            var badge = new LevelInheritanceBadge(TestId);
            Assert.IsTrue(LevelInheritanceBadgeText.TryBuild(RecipeLevelInheritance.InheritUpToUnlocked(0, Ids(11, 12), 0, Ids(21, 22), 1), out var shown));
            badge.Show(shown);
            rootVisualElement.Add(badge);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();

            Assert.IsFalse(LevelInheritanceBadgeText.TryBuild(RecipeLevelInheritance.None, out _));
            badge.Hide();
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.AreEqual(DisplayStyle.None, badge.resolvedStyle.display);
            Assert.IsNull(rootVisualElement.Q(TestId), "no element carries the testid after Hide");
            Assert.IsNull(badge.Q<Label>());
            LogAssert.NoUnexpectedReceived();
        }

        [Test]
        public void 文はMantineのxsとdimmedでtestidは文の要素が持つ()
        {
            var badge = new LevelInheritanceBadge(TestId);
            Assert.IsTrue(LevelInheritanceBadgeText.TryBuild(RecipeLevelInheritance.InheritUpToUnlocked(0, Ids(11), 0, Ids(21), 1), out var text));
            badge.Show(text);
            rootVisualElement.Add(badge);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();

            // testid は文のラベルが持ち（Web の Text の data-testid）、根は名前を持たない
            // The text label carries the testid (the Web Text data-testid); the root has no name
            Assert.IsTrue(string.IsNullOrEmpty(badge.name));
            var label = badge.Q<Label>(TestId);
            Assert.AreEqual(ResolvePixels("--mantine-font-size-xs"), label.resolvedStyle.fontSize, 0.01f);
            AssertSameColor(ResolveColor("--mantine-color-dimmed"), label.resolvedStyle.color, "dimmed");
        }
    }
}
```

`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui/Components/Recipe/LevelInheritanceBadge/LevelInheritanceBadge.cs`:

```csharp
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Text;
using Client.Ui.State.Text;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Components.Recipe
{
    /// <summary>
    /// レシピ選択行とビューアで共有するレベル継承のバッジ（Web の LevelInheritanceBadge）
    /// Level inheritance badge shared by the recipe selection row and the viewer (Web LevelInheritanceBadge)
    /// </summary>
    /// <remarks>
    /// Web は継承しないとき testid の要素ごと描かない。testid は文のラベルに付け、ラベルは Show で作り Hide で外す（文の無い LocalizedLabel は 1 フレーム後にエラーを出すため）。
    /// The Web renders no testid element without inheritance. The testid sits on the text label, created by Show and removed by Hide (a LocalizedLabel without text reports an error a frame later).
    /// </remarks>
    public sealed class LevelInheritanceBadge : VisualElement
    {
        public const string UssClassName = "level-inheritance-badge";
        private const string ShownUssClassName = "level-inheritance-badge--shown";
        private const string TextUssClassName = "level-inheritance-badge__text";
        private readonly string _testid;

        public LevelInheritanceBadge(string testid)
        {
            _testid = testid;
            AddToClassList(UssClassName);
            pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore;
        }

        public void Show(UiText text)
        {
            // Mantine の Text size="xs" c="dimmed" と同じ字の組
            // Same typography as the Mantine Text size="xs" c="dimmed"
            Clear();
            var label = new LocalizedLabel { name = _testid, pickingMode = PickingMode.Ignore };
            label.AddToClassList(UiTypographyClass.TextXs);
            label.AddToClassList(UiTypographyClass.TextDimmed);
            label.AddToClassList(TextUssClassName);
            label.SetText(text);
            Add(label);
            AddToClassList(ShownUssClassName);
        }

        public void Hide()
        {
            Clear();
            RemoveFromClassList(ShownUssClassName);
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 6: `RecipeListScrollView`（本体・USS・L1）

**Files:**
- Create: `moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui/Components/Controls/RecipeListScrollView/RecipeListScrollView.cs`
- Create: `moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui/Components/Controls/RecipeListScrollView/RecipeListScrollView.uss`
- Modify: `moorestech_client/Assets/UI Toolkit/GameUi/GameUiTheme.tss`（`Components/Controls` 区画の末尾に 1 行）
- Modify: `moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Tests/Ui/Components/Controls/ControlKnobOverrides.uss`（末尾に `.control-recipe-list-machine-probe`・`.control-recipe-list-bleed-probe`）
- Test: `moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Tests/Ui/Components/Controls/RecipeListScrollViewTest.cs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GameScrollView(string testid)`（`--scrollarea-scrollbar-size` をバーの幅に当てる）、Foundation の `UiStack(UiGap gap)`（`AddItem(VisualElement)`・`ClearItems()`。`--ui-gap` を読んで表示中の 2 件目以降の上の margin に当てる。前例 `Screens/Challenge/CurrentHud/CurrentChallengeHudView.cs` と `CurrentChallengeHud.uss` の 2 クラスでの `--ui-gap` の上書き）、`UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadPixels`、Task 2 の `--recipe-list-gap`・既存の `--recipe-entry-bleed`（5px）・`--recipe-list-scrollbar-reserve`（4px）・`--recipe-entry-gap`（10px）・`--machine-recipe-row-gap`（8px）、既存の `ControlKnobOverrides.uss`（自分の `ProbePath`。Task 6 が末尾に 2 クラス足す）
- Produces: `namespace Client.Ui.Components.Controls { public sealed class RecipeListScrollView : VisualElement { public const string UssClassName = "recipe-list-scroll-view"; public RecipeListScrollView(string listTestid); public void SetLeadingContent(VisualElement content); public void ClearLeadingContent(); public void SetEntries(IReadOnlyList<VisualElement> entries); } }`（列は `UiStack` で `name` は `listTestid`、中の `GameScrollView` は `<listTestid>-scroll`。行の間隔は `UiStack` が表示中の 2 件目以降の上の margin に当て、表示中の先頭の上の margin は USS に任せる。画面が行を隠すときは `display` を直書きせず行ごと `SetEntries` で渡し直す）。画面のレーンへの約束（S4-recipe・S4-machine）: Web の `listGap` は祖先のクラスで `--recipe-list-gap` を上書きする（クラフトは既定のまま、機械のレシピ選択は `var(--machine-recipe-row-gap)`）。Web の `scrollClassName`・`listClassName` に当たる面ごとの規則も、部品の内部クラスを子孫セレクタで塗らず（設計書 §3.2-2）、画面の祖先クラスでこの部品のつまみ（`--recipe-list-gap`・bleed・reserve）を上書きして当てる。今あるつまみで足りない面の規則（例: クリップの逃げ）は、そのレーン（S4-recipe）が部品につまみを足して A 番号で記帳する

- [ ] **Step 1: L1 を書く**

`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Tests/Ui/Components/Controls/RecipeListScrollViewTest.cs`:

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Client.Tests.Ui.TestHost;
using Client.Ui.Components.Controls;
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Layout;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;
using static Client.Tests.Ui.Components.ThemeTokenFile;

namespace Client.Tests.Ui.Components.Controls
{
    /// <summary>
    /// RecipeListScrollView の名前・行の逃げ余白・バーの幅・先頭の内容・行の間隔（L1）
    /// RecipeListScrollView names, row bleed padding, bar width, leading content and row gap (L1)
    /// </summary>
    public class RecipeListScrollViewTest : UiTestHost
    {
        private const string ListTestId = "recipe-list-test";
        private const string ProbePath = "Assets/Scripts/Client.Tests/Ui/Components/Controls/ControlKnobOverrides.uss";

        [Test]
        public void 列の名前はlistTestidでスクロールはその後ろにscrollを付ける()
        {
            var view = Mount(rootVisualElement, 2);
            Assert.IsNotNull(view.Q<GameScrollView>($"{ListTestId}-scroll"));
            Assert.IsInstanceOf<UiStack>(view.Q(ListTestId));
            Assert.AreEqual(2, view.Q(ListTestId).childCount);
        }

        [Test]
        public void 列の余白は四方bleedで右だけbleedとreserveの和()
        {
            // Web の .list の padding: bleed と padding-right: calc(bleed + reserve)
            // Web .list padding: bleed and padding-right: calc(bleed + reserve)
            var list = Mount(rootVisualElement, 2).Q(ListTestId);
            var bleed = ResolvePixels("--recipe-entry-bleed");
            Assert.AreEqual(bleed, list.resolvedStyle.paddingTop, 0.01f);
            Assert.AreEqual(bleed, list.resolvedStyle.paddingBottom, 0.01f);
            Assert.AreEqual(bleed, list.resolvedStyle.paddingLeft, 0.01f);
            Assert.AreEqual(bleed + ResolvePixels("--recipe-list-scrollbar-reserve"), list.resolvedStyle.paddingRight, 0.01f);
        }

        [Test]
        public void 溢れたときのバーの幅はreserveで先頭の内容は列の前に入り外せる()
        {
            var view = Mount(rootVisualElement, 6);
            var scroll = view.Q<GameScrollView>($"{ListTestId}-scroll");
            // 溢れていることを先に確かめる（溢れなければ GameScrollView がバーを display: none にする）
            // Check the overflow first (without it GameScrollView hides the bar with display: none)
            Assert.Greater(scroll.contentContainer.layout.height, scroll.contentViewport.layout.height, "six rows overflow the 100px host");
            Assert.AreEqual(DisplayStyle.Flex, scroll.verticalScroller.resolvedStyle.display, "bar shown on overflow");
            Assert.AreEqual(ResolvePixels("--recipe-list-scrollbar-reserve"), scroll.verticalScroller.worldBound.width, 0.01f);

            // 先頭の内容は同じスクロールの中で列より上に並ぶ
            // Leading content sits above the list inside the same scroller
            var leading = new VisualElement { name = "leading-probe" };
            leading.style.height = 20f;
            view.SetLeadingContent(leading);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.AreEqual(leading.worldBound.yMax, view.Q(ListTestId).worldBound.yMin, 0.5f);
            view.ClearLeadingContent();
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            Assert.IsNull(view.Q("leading-probe"));
        }

        [Test]
        public void 行の間隔は既定で10pxで機械のレシピ選択の上書きでは8px()
        {
            var plain = Mount(rootVisualElement, 2);
            Assert.AreEqual(ResolvePixels("--recipe-entry-gap"), Gap(plain), 0.5f);
            plain.parent.RemoveFromHierarchy();

            var machine = Mount(Probe("control-recipe-list-machine-probe"), 2);
            Assert.AreEqual(ResolvePixels("--machine-recipe-row-gap"), Gap(machine), 0.5f);
        }

        [Test]
        public void bleedを上書きすると四方と右の和が追う()
        {
            var list = Mount(Probe("control-recipe-list-bleed-probe"), 2).Q(ListTestId);
            Assert.AreEqual(7f, list.resolvedStyle.paddingLeft, 0.01f);
            Assert.AreEqual(7f + ResolvePixels("--recipe-list-scrollbar-reserve"), list.resolvedStyle.paddingRight, 0.01f);
        }

        private RecipeListScrollView Mount(VisualElement parent, int entryCount)
        {
            // 高さ 100 の器に載せ、高さ 40 の行を並べる（ビューは flex-grow: 1 で器いっぱいになり、6 行で溢れる。前例 GameScrollViewTest の高さを決めた器）
            // Mount inside a 100px host with 40px rows (the view grows to the host via flex-grow: 1 and six rows overflow; precedent GameScrollViewTest's sized host)
            var host = new VisualElement();
            host.style.height = 100f;
            parent.Add(host);
            var view = new RecipeListScrollView(ListTestId);
            var entries = new List<VisualElement>();
            for (var i = 0; i < entryCount; i++)
            {
                var entry = new VisualElement { name = $"entry-{i}" };
                entry.style.height = 40f;
                entries.Add(entry);
            }
            view.SetEntries(entries);
            host.Add(view);
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            simulate.FrameUpdate();
            return view;
        }

        private VisualElement Probe(string className)
        {
            var sheet = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<StyleSheet>(ProbePath);
            Assert.IsNotNull(sheet, ProbePath);
            var holder = new VisualElement();
            holder.styleSheets.Add(sheet);
            holder.AddToClassList(className);
            rootVisualElement.Add(holder);
            return holder;
        }

        private static float Gap(RecipeListScrollView view)
        {
            return view.Q("entry-1").worldBound.yMin - view.Q("entry-0").worldBound.yMax;
        }
    }
}
```

（`Mount` が 2 フレーム進めるのは、列の `CustomStyleResolvedEvent` で当てた inline の右の余白と、`UiStack` が `--ui-gap` を読んで当てた margin の後のレイアウトを待つため）

`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui/Components/Controls/RecipeListScrollView/RecipeListScrollView.cs`:

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Layout;
using Client.Ui.Foundation.Theme;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Components.Controls
{
    /// <summary>
    /// レシピ行のスクローラ。先頭の内容と行の列を同じスクロールに並べ、行の逃げ余白と間隔を持つ（Web の RecipeListScrollArea）
    /// Scroller for recipe rows; puts leading content and the row list in one scroll and owns the rows' bleed padding and gap (Web RecipeListScrollArea)
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class RecipeListScrollView : VisualElement
    {
        public const string UssClassName = "recipe-list-scroll-view";
        private const string BleedProperty = "--recipe-list-scroll-view-bleed";
        private const string ReserveProperty = "--recipe-list-scroll-view-reserve";

        private readonly VisualElement _leading;
        private readonly UiStack _list;

        public RecipeListScrollView(string listTestid)
        {
            AddToClassList(UssClassName);

            // 先頭の内容と列を 1 つのスクロールに入れる（Web の ScrollArea の子 2 つ）
            // Leading content and the list share one scroll (the Web ScrollArea's two children)
            var scroll = new GameScrollView($"{listTestid}-scroll");
            scroll.AddToClassList("recipe-list-scroll-view__scroll");
            Add(scroll);
            _leading = new VisualElement();
            _leading.AddToClassList("recipe-list-scroll-view__leading");
            scroll.Add(_leading);
            // 行の列は Mantine の Stack の写し UiStack。間隔は USS で --ui-gap を --recipe-list-gap へ上書きする
            // The row list is UiStack (the Mantine Stack); USS overrides --ui-gap with --recipe-list-gap
            _list = new UiStack(UiGap.None) { name = listTestid };
            _list.AddToClassList("recipe-list-scroll-view__list");
            scroll.Add(_list);
            _list.RegisterCallback<CustomStyleResolvedEvent>(_ => OnListStyleResolved());
        }

        public void SetLeadingContent(VisualElement content)
        {
            _leading.Clear();
            _leading.Add(content);
        }

        public void ClearLeadingContent()
        {
            _leading.Clear();
        }

        public void SetEntries(IReadOnlyList<VisualElement> entries)
        {
            _list.ClearItems();
            foreach (var entry in entries) _list.AddItem(entry);
        }

        private void OnListStyleResolved()
        {
            // 右の余白は Web の calc(bleed + reserve)。USS に calc が無いので 2 つのつまみから C# が当てる（A-47）
            // The right padding is the Web calc(bleed + reserve); USS has no calc, so C# derives it from the two knobs (A-47)
            if (UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadPixels(_list.customStyle, BleedProperty, out var bleed) && UssCustomStyleReader.TryReadPixels(_list.customStyle, ReserveProperty, out var reserve))
            {
                _list.style.paddingRight = bleed + reserve;
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui/Components/Controls/RecipeListScrollView/RecipeListScrollView.uss`:

```css
/* web: moorestech_web/webui/src/shared/ui/RecipeListScrollArea/{index.tsx,style.module.css}（土台の .scroll は panelBodyScrollArea と同じで GameScrollView が持つ） */
/* web: moorestech_web/webui/src/shared/ui/RecipeListScrollArea/{index.tsx,style.module.css} (the base .scroll matches panelBodyScrollArea and lives in GameScrollView) */
.recipe-list-scroll-view {
    --scrollarea-scrollbar-size: var(--recipe-list-scrollbar-reserve);
    flex-grow: 1;
    flex-shrink: 1;
    min-height: 0;
}

.recipe-list-scroll-view__scroll {
    flex-grow: 1;
    flex-shrink: 1;
    min-height: 0;
}

/* 枠の四隅の括弧と外周の輪は行の外へ出るので、全周に逃げ余白をとる。右の和は C# が当てる */
/* Corner brackets and the outer ring sit outside each row, so pad every side by the bleed; C# applies the right sum */
.recipe-list-scroll-view__list {
    flex-shrink: 0;
    padding: var(--recipe-entry-bleed);
    --recipe-list-scroll-view-bleed: var(--recipe-entry-bleed);
    --recipe-list-scroll-view-reserve: var(--recipe-list-scrollbar-reserve);
}

/* 行の間隔は UiStack の --ui-gap。2 クラスで Layout.uss の .ui-stack--gap-none に勝つ（前例 CurrentChallengeHud.uss） */
/* The row gap is UiStack's --ui-gap; two classes beat .ui-stack--gap-none in Layout.uss (precedent CurrentChallengeHud.uss) */
.recipe-list-scroll-view .recipe-list-scroll-view__list {
    --ui-gap: var(--recipe-list-gap);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: 見本の組み立てと USS を書く**

`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui.Editor/Gallery/Entries/Recipe/RecipeGallerySampleSheet.cs`:

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Client.Ui.Components.Controls;
using Client.Ui.Components.Recipe;
using Client.Ui.Components.Slots;
using Client.Ui.Editor.Gallery.Entries.Slots;
using Client.Ui.State.Text;
using Mooresmaster.Localization.Generated;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Editor.Gallery.Entries.Recipe
{
    /// <summary>
    /// レシピ族とレシピのボタン・スクロールの見本の組み立て（ギャラリーと Task 8 の並べ比べの撮影が共有する）
    /// Builds samples for the recipe family and the recipe button and scroll (shared by the gallery and the comparison capture)
    /// </summary>
    public static class RecipeGallerySampleSheet
    {
        public const string MachineSelectionKnobClass = "recipe-gallery__machine-selection";
        public const string ResearchKnobClass = "recipe-gallery__research";
        private const string SheetPath = "Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui.Editor/Gallery/Entries/Recipe/RecipeGallerySamples.uss";

        public static VisualElement CreateSampleRoot()
        {
            // 画面の上書きが欠けると Web と比べられないので、ギャラリーを開いた開発者へ知らせて素の並びで続ける
            // Without the screen overrides nothing can be compared, so tell the developer who opened the gallery and continue unstyled
            var root = new VisualElement();
            root.AddToClassList("recipe-gallery");
            var sheet = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<StyleSheet>(SheetPath);
            if (sheet == null)
            {
                Debug.LogError($"[UiGallery] recipe sample sheet not found: {SheetPath}");
                return root;
            }
            root.styleSheets.Add(sheet);
            return root;
        }

        public static VisualElement AddRow(VisualElement parent, string knobClass)
        {
            var row = new VisualElement();
            row.AddToClassList("recipe-gallery__row");
            row.AddToClassList(knobClass);
            parent.Add(row);
            return row;
        }

        public static RecipeRow CreateCraftRow(string testid, int materialCount, int resultCount, bool craftable)
        {
            // Web の CraftRecipeEntry と同じ組: 秒数・保持していない矢印（0）・クラフトボタン
            // Same set as the Web CraftRecipeEntry: duration, the arrow at 0 while not holding, and the craft button
            var row = new RecipeRow(testid, $"{testid}-arrow");
            row.SetDuration(UiText.FromVerbatim("5秒"));
            row.SetSlots(ItemSlots($"{testid}-material", materialCount, 3), ItemSlots($"{testid}-result", resultCount, 1));
            row.SetProgress(0f);
            row.SetAction(CreateCraftButton($"{testid}-craft", craftable));
            return row;
        }

        public static RecipeRow CreateMachineSelectionOilRow(string testid)
        {
            // 参照 4-block-machine-recipe-selection.png の Oil の行: 液体 10 → 液体 1,000・15秒・静止の矢印・操作なし
            // The Oil row of 4-block-machine-recipe-selection.png: fluid 10 to fluid 1,000, 15 seconds, idle arrow, no action
            var input = new FluidAmountSlot($"{testid}-input-fluid-0");
            input.SetFluid(SlotGallerySampleSheet.Icon("gallery-fluid-input", "Oil"), SlotAmountBadge.Show(10));
            var output = new FluidAmountSlot($"{testid}-output-fluid-0");
            output.SetFluid(SlotGallerySampleSheet.Icon("gallery-fluid-output", "Heavy Oil"), SlotAmountBadge.Show(1000));
            var row = new RecipeRow($"{testid}-row", $"{testid}-arrow");
            row.SetDuration(UiText.FromVerbatim("15秒"));
            row.SetSlots(new VisualElement[] { input }, new VisualElement[] { output });
            row.SetIdle();
            return row;
        }

        public static RecipeActionButton CreateCraftButton(string testid, bool enabled)
        {
            var button = new RecipeActionButton(testid, new UiText(LocalizationKeys.Ui.Recipe.CraftButtonLabel), RecipeActionLabelWeight.Medium);
            button.SetEnabled(enabled);
            return button;
        }

        public static RecipeActionButton CreateResearchButton(string testid, bool enabled)
        {
            // 研究の上書き（色・寸法・字）は置く側の祖先の ResearchKnobClass が当てる
            // The research overrides (colors, size, type) come from the ResearchKnobClass ancestor where it is placed
            var button = new RecipeActionButton(testid, new UiText(LocalizationKeys.Ui.Research.Action), RecipeActionLabelWeight.Regular);
            button.SetEnabled(enabled);
            return button;
        }

        public static RecipeRow CreateCraftCompareRow(string testid, float rowWidth, string duration, double[] ownedCounts, double[] requiredCounts, int resultCount, bool craftable)
        {
            // Web の撮影で読んだ行と同じ条件で組む: 素材は所持/必要（不足は減光と赤字）、結果は 1 枠に個数
            // Build with the conditions read from the Web shot: materials show owned/required (shortage dims and reds), the result is one slot with its count
            var materials = new List<VisualElement>();
            for (var i = 0; i < ownedCounts.Length; i++)
            {
                var lacking = ownedCounts[i] < requiredCounts[i];
                var slot = new ItemSlot($"{testid}-material-{i}");
                // ツールチップは撮影に写らないので null（アイテム名のまま）
                // The tooltip never shows in the shot, so null keeps the item name
                slot.SetContent(new ItemSlotContent(SlotGallerySampleSheet.Icon($"{testid}-material-{i}", $"Item {i + 1}"), null, new ItemSlotMarks(false, false, lacking), new ItemSlotShortage(ownedCounts[i], requiredCounts[i], lacking, null), null, null));
                materials.Add(slot);
            }
            var row = new RecipeRow(testid, $"{testid}-arrow");
            row.SetDuration(UiText.FromVerbatim(duration));
            row.SetSlots(materials, ItemSlots($"{testid}-result", 1, resultCount));
            row.SetProgress(0f);
            row.SetAction(CreateCraftButton($"{testid}-craft", craftable));
            row.style.width = rowWidth;
            return row;
        }

        public static VisualElement CreateCompareSheet(float machineRowWidth, RecipeRow craftEnabledRow, RecipeRow craftDisabledRow, Color craftGround, Color researchGround)
        {
            // 並べ比べの対象を縦に積む: 機械のレシピ選択の Oil の行・Web と同じ条件のクラフト行 2 状態・クラフトボタン 3 状態・研究ボタン 2 状態
            // Stack the targets: the machine-selection Oil row, craft rows in 2 states built from the Web conditions, craft buttons in 3 states and research buttons in 2 states
            var root = CreateSampleRoot();
            root.AddToClassList("recipe-gallery--compare");
            var machine = AddRow(root, MachineSelectionKnobClass);
            var oil = CreateMachineSelectionOilRow("compare-machine-oil");
            oil.style.width = machineRowWidth;
            machine.Add(oil);
            // クラフト行は Web のインベントリの中央のビューアの地（参照の撮影から読んだ色）の上に置く
            // Craft rows sit on the Web inventory centre viewer ground read from the reference shots
            AddGroundedRow(root, craftGround).Add(craftEnabledRow);
            AddGroundedRow(root, craftGround).Add(craftDisabledRow);
            var craftButtons = AddRow(root, "recipe-gallery__plain");
            craftButtons.Add(CreateCraftButton("compare-craft-enabled", true));
            craftButtons.Add(CreateCraftButton("compare-craft-disabled", false));
            craftButtons.Add(RecipeGalleryHoverDriver.HoverWhenLaidOut(CreateCraftButton("compare-craft-hover", true)));
            // 研究ボタンは Web の研究の詳細ペインの地（参照の撮影から読んだ色）の上に置き、切り抜きの余白 6px も同じ地にする
            // Research buttons sit on the Web research detail pane ground read from the reference shots, with the 6px crop margin on the same ground
            var researchButtons = AddGroundedRow(root, researchGround);
            researchButtons.AddToClassList(ResearchKnobClass);
            researchButtons.Add(CreateResearchButton("compare-research-enabled", true));
            researchButtons.Add(CreateResearchButton("compare-research-disabled", false));
            return root;
        }

        // 切り抜きの余白 6px まで同じ地になるよう、地の色と 8px の内側の余白を持つ段を足す
        // Add a row with the ground color and 8px inner padding so the 6px crop margin shares the ground
        private static VisualElement AddGroundedRow(VisualElement parent, Color ground)
        {
            var row = AddRow(parent, "recipe-gallery__plain");
            row.style.backgroundColor = ground;
            row.style.paddingTop = 8f;
            row.style.paddingBottom = 8f;
            row.style.paddingLeft = 8f;
            row.style.paddingRight = 8f;
            return row;
        }

        private static IReadOnlyList<VisualElement> ItemSlots(string prefix, int count, int amount)
        {
            var slots = new List<VisualElement>();
            for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                var slot = new ItemSlot($"{prefix}-{i}");
                slot.SetContent(new ItemSlotContent(SlotGallerySampleSheet.Icon($"{prefix}-{i}", $"Item {i + 1}"), amount, new ItemSlotMarks(false, false, false), null, null, null));
                slots.Add(slot);
            }
            return slots;
        }
    }
}
```

（`.recipe-gallery__row > *` の子セレクタは構造の擬似クラスではないので `UiStyleConventionRules` に当たらない。地の色は前例 `SlotGallerySamples.uss` と同じく書かない。撮影の地は Task 8 で `CaptureToPng` の `ground` に参照の地の画素を渡す）

`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui.Editor/Gallery/Entries/Recipe/RecipeGalleryHoverDriver.cs`（`UiRuntimePanelCapture.Render` は外から入力を差し込めないので、見本自身がパネルに載った後でポインタの移動をパネルへ送る。`:hover` を付けるのは本番と同じパネルのディスパッチャ）:

```csharp
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Editor.Gallery.Entries.Recipe
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 撮影の見本をホバーにする。パネルに載り寸法が決まった最初の回に、要素の中心へポインタの移動をパネルへ送る
    /// Puts a capture sample into hover by sending a pointer move to its centre through the panel once it is laid out
    /// </summary>
    public static class RecipeGalleryHoverDriver
    {
        public static T HoverWhenLaidOut<T>(T target) where T : VisualElement
        {
            // 載った時点では worldBound が 0 なので、寸法の決まる回を待って送る
            // worldBound is still zero on attach, so wait for the layout pass before sending
            target.RegisterCallback<AttachToPanelEvent>(_ => target.RegisterCallback<GeometryChangedEvent>(OnGeometryChanged));
            return target;

            #region Internal

            void OnGeometryChanged(GeometryChangedEvent evt)
            {
                // silent-ok: 寸法 0 の回は中心が無いので次の回を待つ / ホバーが付かなければ Task 8 Step 3 の面の色の照合が止める
                // silent-ok: a zero-size pass has no centre so wait for the next / Task 8 Step 3 stops on the face colors if hover never applies
                if (target.worldBound.width <= 0f) return;
                target.UnregisterCallback<GeometryChangedEvent>(OnGeometryChanged);
                SendPointerMove(target.panel, target.worldBound.center);
            }

            #endregion
        }

        private static void SendPointerMove(IPanel panel, Vector2 panelPosition)
        {
            // 本番の入力と同じくディスパッチャが要素の下のポインタを更新して :hover を付ける
            // As with real input, the dispatcher updates the element under the pointer and applies :hover
            var systemEvent = new Event { type = EventType.MouseMove, mousePosition = panelPosition };
            using var move = PointerMoveEvent.GetPooled(systemEvent);
            panel.visualTree.SendEvent(move);
        }
    }
}
```

`moorestech_client/Assets/Scripts/Client.Ui.Editor/Gallery/Entries/Recipe/RecipeGalleryCompareList.cs`:

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Client.Ui.Components.Controls;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Editor.Gallery.Entries.Recipe
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 並べ比べのレシピ列。機械のレシピ選択の上書きを当てた列に、名前の段と Oil の行の項目を数本積む（バー・逃げ余白・項目の間隔を比べる）
    /// Comparison recipe column; stacks name-band plus Oil-row entries in a column with the machine-selection overrides (to compare the bar, bleed margins and entry gap)
    /// </summary>
    public static class RecipeGalleryCompareList
    {
        public const string EntryNameClass = "recipe-gallery__entry-name";

        public static VisualElement Create(float columnWidth, float columnHeight, int rowCount)
        {
            // 列の寸法は参照の列から読んだ値。行は Web より多く積んでスクロールのバーを出す
            // The column size comes from the reference column; stack more rows than fit so the scrollbar shows
            var root = RecipeGallerySampleSheet.CreateSampleRoot();
            var column = new VisualElement();
            column.AddToClassList(RecipeGallerySampleSheet.MachineSelectionKnobClass);
            column.style.width = columnWidth;
            column.style.height = columnHeight;
            var list = new RecipeListScrollView("compare-machine-list");
            list.style.flexGrow = 1;
            // Web の 1 項目は「名前 → 継承バッジ → RecipeRow」なので、名前の段（12px・行高 1・下 4px）を行の上に置く。参照にバッジは写っていない
            // A Web entry is name, inheritance badge, then RecipeRow, so put the name band (12px, line-height 1, 4px below) above the row; no badge shows in the reference
            var entries = new List<VisualElement>();
            for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++)
            {
                var entry = new VisualElement();
                var name = new Label("Oil");
                name.AddToClassList(EntryNameClass);
                entry.Add(name);
                entry.Add(RecipeGallerySampleSheet.CreateMachineSelectionOilRow($"compare-machine-list-{i}"));
                entries.Add(entry);
            }
            list.SetEntries(entries);
            column.Add(list);
            root.Add(column);
            return root;
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
using Client.Localization;
using Client.Ui.Components.Recipe;
using Client.Ui.State.Text;
using Mooresmaster.Localization.Generated;
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Editor.Gallery.Entries.Recipe
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 並べ比べの継承バッジ 2 種（継承・上限つき継承）。Web の機械のレシピ選択のバッジと同じ幅で並べ、文の段組を比べる
    /// Comparison inheritance badges (inherit and capped); laid out at the Web machine-selection badge width to compare the text layout
    /// </summary>
    public static class RecipeGalleryCompareBadges
    {
        public static VisualElement Create(float width, string cappedLevel)
        {
            var root = RecipeGallerySampleSheet.CreateSampleRoot();
            root.Add(CreateBadge("compare-badge-inherit", width, new UiText(LocalizationKeys.Ui.Recipe.LevelInheritance)));
            root.Add(CreateBadge("compare-badge-capped", width, new UiText(LocalizationKeys.Ui.Recipe.LevelInheritanceCapped, new[] { new LocalizationTextParam("level", cappedLevel) })));
            return root;
        }

        // Web の spec の幅で置き、撮影の切り抜きが重ならないよう下に間をあける
        // Place at the Web spec width with a gap below so the crops never overlap
        private static LevelInheritanceBadge CreateBadge(string testid, float width, UiText text)
        {
            var badge = new LevelInheritanceBadge(testid);
            badge.style.width = width;
            badge.style.marginBottom = 16f;
            badge.Show(text);
            return badge;
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
using UnityEngine.UIElements;

namespace Client.Ui.Editor.Gallery.Entries.Recipe
{
    // RecipeRowの見本（クラフト行・3点の折り返し・機械のレシピ選択の液体の行）
    // RecipeRow samples (craft row, three-slot wrap, machine-selection fluid row)
    public sealed class RecipeRowGalleryEntry : IUiGalleryEntry
    {
        public UiGalleryFamily Family => UiGalleryFamily.Recipe;
        public string ComponentName => "RecipeRow";

        public VisualElement CreateSample()
        {
            var root = RecipeGallerySampleSheet.CreateSampleRoot();
            AddSized(RecipeGallerySampleSheet.AddRow(root, "recipe-gallery__plain"), RecipeGallerySampleSheet.CreateCraftRow("gallery-recipe-craft", 2, 1, true));
            AddSized(RecipeGallerySampleSheet.AddRow(root, "recipe-gallery__plain"), RecipeGallerySampleSheet.CreateCraftRow("gallery-recipe-wrap", 3, 3, false));
            AddSized(RecipeGallerySampleSheet.AddRow(root, RecipeGallerySampleSheet.MachineSelectionKnobClass), RecipeGallerySampleSheet.CreateMachineSelectionOilRow("gallery-recipe-machine"));
            return root;

            #region Internal

            // 見本の幅はクラフトパネルの本文に近い 480px
            // Sample width is 480px, close to the craft panel body
            void AddSized(VisualElement parent, VisualElement row)
            {
                row.style.width = 480f;
                parent.Add(row);
            }

            #endregion
        }
    }
}
```
